SELECT [t].[Id], LTRIM(RTRIM([t].[Name] + N' ' + [t].[SeasonName])), [t].[MangaThumbnail], [t].[MangaBackground], [t].[LastChapterId], [t].[LastChapterIndex], COALESCE([t].[LastChapterUpdate], '0001-01-01T00:00:00.0000000'), CASE
    WHEN [t0].[Id] IS NULL THEN CAST(0 AS bigint)
    ELSE [t0].[TotalCount]
END, COALESCE((
    SELECT AVG([r].[Rating])
    FROM [Rating] AS [r]
    WHERE ([r].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [r].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [r].[DeleteDate] IS NULL AND [t].[Id] = [r].[ToMangaId]), 0.0E0), [t0].[Id]
FROM (
    SELECT [m].[Id], [m].[LastChapterId], [m].[LastChapterIndex], [m].[LastChapterUpdate], [m].[MangaBackground], [m].[MangaThumbnail], [m].[Name], [m].[SeasonName]
    FROM [Manga] AS [m]
    WHERE ([m].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [m].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [m].[DeleteDate] IS NULL
    ORDER BY [m].[LastChapterUpdate] DESC, [m].[Id] DESC
    OFFSET @__p_0 ROWS FETCH NEXT @__p_1 ROWS ONLY
) AS [t]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [v].[Id], [v].[MangaId], [v].[TotalCount]
    FROM [ViewCount] AS [v]
    WHERE ([v].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [v].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [v].[DeleteDate] IS NULL
) AS [t0] ON [t].[Id] = [t0].[MangaId]
ORDER BY [t].[LastChapterUpdate] DESC, [t].[Id] DESC, [t0].[Id]

    FROM [ReadingProgress] AS [r]
    WHERE ([r].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [r].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [r].[DeleteDate] IS NULL AND [r].[UserId] = @__request_UserId_0
    GROUP BY [r].[MangaId]
    ORDER BY MAX([r].[LastReadAt]) DESC
    OFFSET @__p_1 ROWS FETCH NEXT @__p_2 ROWS ONLY
) AS [t]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [t1].[MangaId], [t1].[MangaName], [t1].[MangaThumbnail], [t1].[ChapterId], [t1].[ChapterTitle], [t1].[ChapterIndex], [t1].[LastPage], [t1].[LastReadAt], [t1].[c]
    FROM (
        SELECT [r0].[MangaId], [t0].[Name] AS [MangaName], [t0].[MangaThumbnail], [r0].[ChapterId], [t3].[Title] AS [ChapterTitle], [t3].[Index] AS [ChapterIndex], [r0].[LastPage], [r0].[LastReadAt], 1 AS [c], ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY [r0].[MangaId] ORDER BY [r0].[LastReadAt] DESC) AS [row]
        FROM [ReadingProgress] AS [r0]
        INNER JOIN (
            SELECT [m].[Id], [m].[MangaThumbnail], [m].[Name]
            FROM [Manga] AS [m]
            WHERE ([m].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [m].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [m].[DeleteDate] IS NULL
        ) AS [t0] ON [r0].[MangaId] = [t0].[Id]
        INNER JOIN (
            SELECT [c].[Id], [c].[Index], [c].[Title]
            FROM [Chapter] AS [c]
            WHERE ([c].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [c].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [c].[DeleteDate] IS NULL
        ) AS [t3] ON [r0].[ChapterId] = [t3].[Id]
        WHERE ([r0].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [r0].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [r0].[DeleteDate] IS NULL AND [r0].[UserId] = @__request_UserId_0
    ) AS [t1]
    WHERE [t1].[row] <= 1
) AS [t2] ON [t].[MangaId] = [t2].[MangaId]
ORDER BY [t].[c] DESC

SELECT COUNT(*)
FROM [Manga] AS [m]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [v].[Id], [v].[BlogId], [v].[ChapterId], [v].[CommentId], [v].[CreateDate], [v].[DayCount], [v].[DeleteDate], [v].[IdUserCreate], [v].[IdUserDelete], [v].[IdUserUpdate], [v].[LastDateModify], [v].[LastMonthModify], [v].[LastYearModify], [v].[MangaId], [v].[MonthCount], [v].[TotalCount], [v].[UpdateDate], [v].[YearCount]
    FROM [ViewCount] AS [v]
    WHERE ([v].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [v].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [v].[DeleteDate] IS NULL
) AS [t] ON [m].[Id] = [t].[MangaId]
WHERE ([m].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [m].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [m].[DeleteDate] IS NULL

SELECT [m].[Id], LTRIM(RTRIM([m].[Name] + N' ' + [m].[SeasonName])) AS [DisplayName], COALESCE([m].[MangaThumbnail], N'') AS [MangaThumbnail], CASE
    WHEN [t].[Id] IS NULL THEN CAST(0 AS bigint)
    ELSE [t].[TotalCount]
END AS [View]
FROM [Manga] AS [m]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [v].[Id], [v].[MangaId], [v].[TotalCount]
    FROM [ViewCount] AS [v]
    WHERE ([v].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [v].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [v].[DeleteDate] IS NULL
) AS [t] ON [m].[Id] = [t].[MangaId]
WHERE ([m].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [m].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [m].[DeleteDate] IS NULL
ORDER BY CASE
    WHEN [t].[Id] IS NULL THEN CAST(0 AS bigint)
    ELSE [t].[TotalCount]
END DESC, [m].[Id] DESC
OFFSET @__p_0 ROWS FETCH NEXT @__p_1 ROWS ONLY
